country_event = {
	id = cosmo_royalprocure_events.4

	picture = cosmogfx_MERCHANT_AFRICA_EUROPE_1_eventPicture

		ai = no
	}

			hidden_effect = {				
				}

	option = {#rajput
		name = cosmo_royalprocure_events.4.a
		trigger = {

				culture_group = french

				culture_group = byzantine

			}	
	picture = MERCHANTS_ARGUING_eventPicture
	}

	trigger = {

		culture_group = maghrebi		
	}

	}

# Barbary Menu 1
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_royalprocure_events.11
	title = cosmo_royalprocure_events.11.t
	desc = cosmo_royalprocure_events.11.d
	picture = cosmogfx_CONCUBINE_BARBARY_MENU_1_eventPicture


	is_triggered_only = yes

		
	
	option = {#A
		name = cosmo_royalprocure_events.11.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_owned_province = {
					has_province_modifier = barbary_concubine_1a
				}
			}
			}
			#get concubine
			add_years_of_income = -0.1
			add_estate_influence_modifier = {
				estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
				desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
				influence = -20
				duration = 3650
			}
			add_estate_influence_modifier = {
				estate = estate_harem
				desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
				influence = -20
				duration = 3650
			}
			add_estate_influence_modifier = {
				estate = estate_ooku
				desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
				influence = -20
				duration = 3650
			}		
			add_estate_influence_modifier = {
				estate = estate_nobleaffairs
				desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
				influence = -20
				duration = 3650
			}
			add_estate_influence_modifier = {
				estate = estate_wives
				desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_EUROPE
				influence = -20
				duration = 3650
			}		
			set_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
			capital_scope = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}

				name = barbary_concubine_1a

				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_already_purchased_tt
		}					   																					
	}

				name = southsea_concubine_1c
				duration =  -1
				#desc = southsea_concubine_1c_DESC
				}				
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_owned_province = {
					has_province_modifier = southsea_concubine_1c
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = cosmo_already_purchased_tt
		}					   																					
	}
	option = {#Next Menu
		name = cosmo_royalprocure_events.14.7			
		country_event = {#next menu
			id = cosmo_royalprocure_events.141
			}	   																					
	}			
}

# South Sea Menu 2
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_royalprocure_events.999
	title = cosmo_royalprocure_events.999.t
	desc = cosmo_royalprocure_events.999.d
	picture = cosmogfx_CONCUBINE_BARBARY_MENU_2_eventPicture


	is_triggered_only = yes

		
	
	option = {#A
		name = cosmo_royalprocure_events.999.a	
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
				estate = estate_nobleaffairs
				desc = COSMO_NOBLEAFFAIR_INFLUENCE_FIRST
				influence = 10
				duration = 1095
			}					   																					
	}
